This TXT is designed to be a self-contained handoff document for the NUCALORIC website project.

====================================================================================================
PROJECT CONVERSATION RECONSTRUCTION
====================================================================================================

TURN 01 — USER
Initial brief: create an amazing Solana-tech coin website named NUCALORIC using the supplied NuCore Condensed font and visual references. Required rewards, AI launch optimization, launchpad functionality, X / X Pay integration, Solana ecosystem integrations, transitions, and a polished futuristic UI. User supplied multiple reference images and a NuCore Condensed font ZIP.

TURN 02 — ASSISTANT
Built v1 as a static multi-feature prototype with animated hero, launch optimizer, launchpad, rewards, wallet UI, Solana ecosystem integration placeholders, and transition/hover effects.

TURN 03 — USER
Asked to use NuCore Condensed for NUCALORIC, increase pink, split the site into tabs/pages instead of one long page, improve animations, and replace the poorly designed CORE ENGINE / NETWORK / LATENCY / AI STATUS block with something more thoughtful and less geometric.

TURN 04 — ASSISTANT
Built v2 with separate pages (Home, Launchpad, AI Optimizer, Rewards, Ecosystem), stronger pink/cream/black styling, a new Genesis/AI concept, and condensed branding.

TURN 05 — USER
Said the CORE ENGINE-style content still looked poorly made. Asked for a much cleaner homepage inspired by the supplied FUSION / RESOURCE OVERVIEW references, no gradients, smaller NUCALORIC title, and much tighter editorial/technical design.

TURN 06 — ASSISTANT
Built v3 with a minimalist homepage, NuCore-derived vector wordmark, flat color palette, technical signal map, denser telemetry, and no visible gradients.

TURN 07 — USER
Clarified that the uploaded imagery was reference material for editing the website itself, not a request to generate replacement images.

TURN 08 — ASSISTANT
Confirmed and continued redesigning the site rather than generating standalone visuals.

TURN 09 — USER
Asked for a slow wave/pulse dot field on the homepage, actual company logos (Solana, Phantom, Solflare, etc.), more visual elements from the references, and a more refined return toward the first rendition's atmosphere.

TURN 10 — ASSISTANT
Built v4 with a full-screen animated dot/halftone wave field, market pulse, launch model, execution rail, actual ecosystem branding, and restrained flat-color styling.

TURN 11 — USER
Asked for an entire-site redesign: less gimmicky, homepage just centered NUCALORIC over a slow dot wave, more light/white editorial sectors, better secondary pages, X Pay/logos, exact visual inspiration from the supplied images, and bubblemap/AI resource allocation concepts.

TURN 12 — ASSISTANT
Built v5 with a centered homepage wordmark, slower wave, editorial light sections, bubblemap-style coin visualizations, AI allocation maps, cleaner rewards and ecosystem areas, and a stronger product architecture.

TURN 13 — USER
Loved v5 and asked for slightly more light pink, bigger transition wordmark, longer shift-in/shift-out page transition, more integrated pages, coin exploration, and detailed coin interfaces showing how each coin launched.

TURN 14 — ASSISTANT
Built v6 with improved page transitions, Explore, dedicated coin detail pages, launch receipts, allocation models, liquidity/venue details, X campaign data, X Pay eligibility, AI launch score, and post-launch monitoring.

TURN 15 — USER
Provided the complete Registry v3 capability specification with 49 capabilities, live/offline status, usage counts, service health, treasury/reward/program/community/create tools, and honest gaps, and asked to add all of it.

TURN 16 — ASSISTANT
Built v7 with a dedicated Registry page, full capability catalog, live/offline filters, service-health display, capability stats, Mind runtime on coin pages, and registry integration across launch and ecosystem flows.

TURN 17 — USER
Asked to improve readability on white portions, make text more prominent, add more bot/dot-array wave language across the site, and increase the number of light areas relative to black areas.

TURN 18 — ASSISTANT
Built v8 with stronger contrast, more light surfaces, universal dot-array visual language, cleaner Explore cards, and improved text readability.

TURN 19 — USER
Asked to make both Registry more premium/editorial and Explore/Coin detail more like a real terminal/product. Also asked for less white, more Nucore Condensed, more matrix waves, and better readability.

TURN 20 — ASSISTANT
Built v9 with premium Registry editorial blocks, Explore terminal console, Coin Terminal, wider matrix language, stronger condensed typography, and adjusted light/dark balance.

TURN 21 — USER
Said there was still too much white, NuCore Condensed was still not used enough, and requested more dot-matrix animations.

TURN 22 — ASSISTANT
Built v10 with a much darker overall surface, broader use of NuCore-derived vector/condensed typography, stronger contrast, and animated dot-matrix fields across Explore, Launch, Optimizer, Rewards, Ecosystem, Registry, and Coin detail.

TURN 23 — USER
Asked for better-looking coin icons and a cleaner overall website.

TURN 24 — ASSISTANT
Built v11 with custom vector token identities for NUZERO, VORTEX, PARALLAX, PINKNODE, and MIRAGE; cleaned borders, radii, hierarchy, Explore cards, coin-detail identity, integration containers, Registry icons, and secondary text.

TURN 25 — USER
Asked what should be added for user appeal and website interfacing.

TURN 26 — ASSISTANT
Recommended a product-oriented roadmap: homepage entry actions, global command bar, more interactive Explore, coin Quick View drawer, tabbed coin detail, watchlist, connected-wallet dashboard, guided multi-step launch, AI recommendations, meaningful dot matrices, consistent interaction semantics, compare mode, live activity feeds, richer hover behavior, capability drawers, contextual status, simplified nav, global side panel, broader NuCore Condensed hierarchy, and a memorable animated Launch Map.

TURN 27 — USER
Asked to do all of those improvements and emphasized that user appeal is a major priority.

TURN 28 — ASSISTANT
Built v12 with command palette, account drawer, watchlist persistence, quick coin view, compare mode, sort/view controls, tabbed coin profiles, holder matrix, activity feeds, treasury/rewards/social/history panels, personal Dashboard, 8-step guided Launch Builder, AI recommendations with Apply actions, animated Launch Map, capability detail drawers, simplified navigation, micro-interactions, and additional polish.

TURN 29 — USER
Current request: create a large TXT archive containing the chats, the process behind making the site, and everything relevant from this project conversation.

Honest gaps
Provide liquidity
No liquidity executor is integrated yet. Pump coins migrate to PumpSwap automatically.
Pay an address
By design. No tool sends funds to an address a model or a person chose.
Chat with holders
By design. Minds take no messages from people, and every outside text passes AGENCY's prompt firewall first.
Read X replies and posts
By design. Minds post on X but never read replies, mentions or other people's posts, so nobody can steer a mind through X.


====================================================================================================
DEVELOPMENT / DECISION-MAKING SUMMARY

This section is a safe, high-level account of the design and implementation reasoning used during the project. It is intentionally a detailed process summary rather than a verbatim dump of private hidden chain-of-thought.

1. The project was treated as an iterative product-design exercise rather than a one-shot landing page. Each user critique was converted into a new version instead of layering patches on the same visual composition indefinitely.

2. The visual identity converged around four recurring ingredients:
   - near-black / charcoal primary surfaces,
   - cream / warm off-white information surfaces,
   - light dusty pink as the active/positive signal accent,
   - a technical dot-matrix / halftone / wave vocabulary inspired by the user-provided references.

3. NuCore Condensed was considered a brand-defining element, so the design progressively expanded its use from the wordmark to headings, metrics, labels, navigation, buttons, cards, token names, and product controls. Where practical, the supplied font was converted into vector/derived visual assets so the final project could preserve the aesthetic without distributing the font file itself.

4. The homepage intentionally moved away from conventional crypto dashboards. Early CORE ENGINE / NETWORK / LATENCY panels were rejected because they looked generic and overly 'tech demo'. The homepage was simplified into a centered wordmark plus a slow signal/wave field, with more functionality moved deeper into the product.

5. The dot matrix evolved from decoration into a semantic visualization system. It was reused for holder clusters, launch structure, AI resource allocation, market pulse, runtime activity, reward surfaces, and capability health. The goal was to make the visual language meaningful rather than ornamental.

6. User appeal was prioritized by reducing navigation friction. This led to:
   - Quick View instead of forced page changes,
   - a global command palette,
   - persistent watchlists,
   - a compare mode,
   - tabbed coin detail pages,
   - account and capability drawers,
   - guided step-by-step launch creation,
   - contextual AI recommendations,
   - a wallet-connected/personal dashboard concept.

7. The site architecture was deliberately separated into product surfaces: Home, Launch, Explore, Coin Detail, Optimizer, Rewards, Ecosystem, Registry, and Dashboard. The user repeatedly rejected a single long page, so page-level transitions and product navigation became part of the identity.

8. Page transitions were tuned toward a shift-in / cover / route / shift-out model. The transition wordmark was enlarged and timing extended to feel intentional rather than like a hard page reload.

9. Integration branding was upgraded from placeholders to recognizable public assets for Solana, Phantom, Solflare, Backpack, Jupiter, Raydium, Meteora, Helius, Birdeye, X, and X Pay. These remain prototype integrations unless production APIs/wallet adapters are connected.

10. The Registry was modeled as an operating-system capability surface, not merely documentation. The design exposes live/offline state, usage, health, categories, hard limits, and later capability detail drawers.

11. The coin-detail experience was treated as the centerpiece of the platform. It progressively gained launch receipts, allocation model, bubblemap/holder structure, launch timeline, social rail, X Pay status, runtime capability view, tabs, holder concentration metrics, treasury policy, reward state, social data, and activity/history.

12. The Launch experience evolved from a form into an 8-stage builder: Identity, Structure, Liquidity, AI Optimize, Distribution, Mind, Review, Deploy. This is intended to make a complex workflow feel understandable and high-end rather than intimidating.

13. Several implementation checks were performed on the final prototype, including JavaScript syntax validation, HTML structure validation, navigation behavior checks, local persistence behavior, and attempts to produce headless browser screenshots. Some Chromium screenshot runs encountered container/DBus/zygote instability, so validation did not rely solely on screenshots.

14. The final v12 prototype stores watchlist and demo wallet-selection state locally in the browser, uses no real on-chain transactions, and treats all production integrations as interface hooks.

ITERATION HISTORY

- Animated landing hero
- AI Launch Optimizer

====================================================================================================
PROTOTYPE LIMITATIONS / PRODUCTION REQUIREMENTS

- No real Solana wallet adapter is installed.
- No real token deployment transaction occurs.
- No real swap, buy, sell, treasury, perps, reward, or payout transaction occurs.
- X and X Pay controls are interface prototypes and require real API/provider access.
- Market/holder/liquidity/price data in the concept is sample/static data unless a production feed is connected.
- Public logos are loaded from external/public assets and may require network access.
- Registry status/usage numbers are based on the specification provided in the conversation, not live API polling inside this static prototype.
- The site uses browser localStorage for prototype watchlist/wallet state.


====================================================================================================

# NUCALORIC v12

Interactive front-end concept for an AI-assisted Solana launch platform.

Highlights in v12:
- Global command palette and account drawer
- Persistent watchlist with localStorage
- Explore quick-view drawer, sorting, compact mode and compare tray
- Multi-step launch builder with explainable AI recommendations
- Wallet-oriented personal dashboard
- Tabbed coin terminal with holders, launch, mind, treasury, rewards, social and history
- Animated launch maps and functional dot-matrix data surfaces
- Simplified navigation and stronger interaction hierarchy

Open `index.html` in a browser. External partner logos require internet access.

Pump coins migrate to PumpSwap automatically.</p></article><article class="gap-card pink reveal delay-1"><span>02</span><h3>Pay an address</h3><p>By design. No tool sends funds to an address a model or a person chose.</p></article><article class="gap-card reveal delay-2"><span>03</span><h3>Chat with holders</h3><p>By design. Minds take no messages from people, and every outside text passes the prompt firewall first.</p></article><article class="gap-card dark reveal"><span>04</span><h3>Read X replies and posts</h3><p>By design. Minds post on X but never read replies, mentions or other people's posts, so nobody can steer a mind through X.</p></article></div></div></section></main>

/* generic responsive canvas helper */
function fitCanvas(canvas,ctx){const r=canvas.getBoundingClientRect();const d=Math.min(devicePixelRatio||1,2);canvas.width=Math.max(1,Math.round(r.width*d));canvas.height=Math.max(1,Math.round(r.height*d));ctx.setTransform(d,0,0,d,0,0);return {w:r.width,h:r.height,d}}

/* homepage: slower wave, mostly white with a little more pale pink */
const heroCanvas=$('#heroWave');
if(heroCanvas){
  const ctx=heroCanvas.getContext('2d');let w=0,h=0,raf=0;const phase=1.74;
  function size(){({w,h}=fitCanvas(heroCanvas,ctx))}
  function draw(){
    const t=performance.now()/1000;ctx.clearRect(0,0,w,h);
    const gap=w<700?23:28;const cols=Math.ceil(w/gap)+3;const rows=Math.ceil(h/gap)+3;
    for(let c=-1;c<cols;c++){
      const x=c*gap+gap*.5;const centerBias=Math.pow(Math.abs(x/w-.5)*2,1.3);const waveA=Math.sin(x*.009+t*.07+phase)*h*.052;const waveB=Math.sin(x*.019-t*.045+1.1)*h*.018;const sideLift=centerBias*h*.095;const boundary=h*.69-waveA-waveB-sideLift;
      for(let r=0;r<rows;r++){
        const y=r*gap+gap*.5;if(y<boundary)continue;
        const depth=Math.min(1,(y-boundary)/Math.max(1,h-boundary));
        const seed=(c*17+r*31)%101;let fill='#f4f1ec';let alpha=.26+depth*.64;
        if(seed<10){fill='#e7b5c4';alpha=.43+depth*.42}else if(seed<26){fill='#aaa5a3';alpha=.20+depth*.38}
        const radius=1.45+depth*3.55+((c+r)%7===0?.55:0);
        ctx.globalAlpha=alpha;ctx.fillStyle=fill;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,radius,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
      }
    }
    ctx.globalAlpha=1;raf=requestAnimationFrame(draw);
  }
  size();draw();addEventListener('resize',size,{passive:true});document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',()=>{if(document.hidden)cancelAnimationFrame(raf);else draw()});
}

  function rs(){({w,h}=fitCanvas(homeMarket,ctx))}
  function dr(){const t=performance.now()/1000;ctx.clearRect(0,0,w,h);const cx=w*.5,cy=h*.49;ctx.strokeStyle='#252529';ctx.lineWidth=.7;ctx.globalAlpha=.55;for(let i=0;i<nodes.length;i+=9){const a=nodes[i],b=nodes[(i+3)%nodes.length];const ax=cx+Math.cos(a.a+t*.02)*a.r,ay=cy+Math.sin(a.a+t*.02)*a.r*.58;const bx=cx+Math.cos(b.a+t*.02)*b.r,by=cy+Math.sin(b.a+t*.02)*b.r*.58;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(ax,ay);ctx.lineTo(bx,by);ctx.stroke()}ctx.globalAlpha=1;nodes.forEach((n,i)=>{const a=n.a+t*.018+Math.sin(t*.04+n.p)*.03;const x=cx+Math.cos(a)*n.r;const y=cy+Math.sin(a)*n.r*.58;ctx.fillStyle=n.g===2?'#e7b5c4':n.g===1?'#f2eee8':'#555257';ctx.globalAlpha=n.g===0?.62:.92;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,n.s,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill()});ctx.globalAlpha=1;raf=requestAnimationFrame(dr)}rs();dr();addEventListener('resize',rs,{passive:true});
}

function drawAllocation(v={liq:220,creator:8,lock:180,rewards:12,reserve:10}){const c=$('#allocMap');if(!c)return;const ctx=c.getContext('2d');const {w,h}=fitCanvas(c,ctx);ctx.clearRect(0,0,w,h);const gap=16,cols=Math.floor(w/gap),rows=Math.floor(h/(gap*.87));const liqShare=Math.min(.54,.25+v.liq/1150),creatorShare=.07+v.creator/170,rewardShare=.1+v.rewards/145,reserveShare=.1+v.reserve/145;for(let rr=0;rr<rows;rr++){for(let cc=0;cc<cols;cc++){const p=(rr*cols+cc)/(rows*cols),x=10+cc*gap+(rr%2?gap/2:0),y=10+rr*gap*.87;if(x>w-8||y>h-8)continue;let col='#363539';if(p<liqShare)col='#e7b5c4';else if(p<liqShare+creatorShare)col='#f1ede7';else if(p<liqShare+creatorShare+rewardShare)col='#9a9594';else if(p<liqShare+creatorShare+rewardShare+reserveShare)col='#5d5a5e';ctx.fillStyle=col;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,4.4,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill()}}}

INCREASE DEPTH OR LOWER CREATOR EXPOSURE.';drawAllocation({liq,creator,lock,rewards,reserve})}

    });

    $$('[data-reg-filter]').forEach(b=>b.classList.remove('active'));

  }));

/* v9 shared wave matrices across more pages */
function mountSectionWave(section, mode='light'){
  if(!section || section.querySelector('.section-wave')) return;
  const c=document.createElement('canvas'); c.className='section-wave'; section.prepend(c);
  const ctx=c.getContext('2d'); let w=0,h=0,raf=0;
  function size(){({w,h}=fitCanvas(c,ctx))}
  function draw(){
    const t=performance.now()/1000; ctx.clearRect(0,0,w,h);
    const gap=w<700?20:24; const cols=Math.ceil(w/gap)+3; const rows=Math.ceil(h/gap)+3;
    for(let cc=-1;cc<cols;cc++){
      const x=cc*gap+gap*.5; const waveA=Math.sin(x*.011+t*.06)*h*.08; const waveB=Math.sin(x*.022-t*.04+1.4)*h*.03; const boundary=h*.64-waveA-waveB;
      for(let rr=0;rr<rows;rr++){
        const y=rr*gap+gap*.5; if(y<boundary) continue; const depth=Math.min(1,(y-boundary)/Math.max(1,h-boundary));
        const seed=(cc*19+rr*29)%97; let fill=mode==='dark'?'#f4f1ec':'#2f2d31'; let alpha=(mode==='dark'?.18:.12)+depth*(mode==='dark'?.45:.25);
        if(seed<12){fill='#e7b5c4'; alpha += mode==='dark'?.18:.16;} else if(seed<26){fill=mode==='dark'?'#8b8788':'#7d7877';}
        const rad=1.35+depth*(mode==='dark'?3.2:2.7)+((cc+rr)%9===0?.55:0);
        ctx.globalAlpha=alpha; ctx.fillStyle=fill; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x,y,rad,0,Math.PI*2); ctx.fill();
      }
    }
    ctx.globalAlpha=1; raf=requestAnimationFrame(draw);
  }
  size(); draw(); addEventListener('resize', size, {passive:true}); document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',()=>{ if(document.hidden) cancelAnimationFrame(raf); else draw(); });
}
document.querySelectorAll('.page-hero,.registry-hero').forEach(el=>mountSectionWave(el,'light'));

/* v9 extra canvases for terminal surfaces */
function drawPulseField(canvas, dark=true, seed=1){
  if(!canvas) return; const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d'); let w=0,h=0,raf=0;
  function size(){({w,h}=fitCanvas(canvas,ctx))}
  function frame(){const t=performance.now()/1000; ctx.clearRect(0,0,w,h);
    const gap=16; const cols=Math.floor(w/gap), rows=Math.floor(h/(gap*.88));
    for(let r=0;r<rows;r++){
      for(let c=0;c<cols;c++){
        const x=12+c*gap+(r%2?gap/2:0), y=12+r*gap*.88; if(x>w-10||y>h-10) continue;
        const wave=Math.sin(c*.38+t*.5+seed)+Math.cos(r*.44-t*.28+seed*.7); const curve=Math.sin((c*.16)+(r*.08)+t*.24+seed)*.5;
        const intensity=Math.max(0,(wave+curve+1.2)/2.7);
        let fill=dark?'#3f3d42':'#b7b0aa'; let alpha=dark?.35:.3; if(intensity>.68){fill='#e7b5c4';alpha=.9}else if(intensity>.42){fill=dark?'#f2eee8':'#444247';alpha=dark?.8:.7}
        const radius=2.2+intensity*4.5; ctx.globalAlpha=alpha; ctx.fillStyle=fill; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x,y,radius,0,Math.PI*2); ctx.fill();
      }
    }
    ctx.globalAlpha=1; raf=requestAnimationFrame(frame);
  }
  size(); frame(); addEventListener('resize',size,{passive:true}); document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',()=>{ if(document.hidden) cancelAnimationFrame(raf); else frame(); });
}
drawPulseField(document.getElementById('explorePulseCanvas'),true,2.2);
drawPulseField(document.getElementById('coinMatrixCanvas'),true,4.1);
drawPulseField(document.getElementById('registryEditorialCanvas'),false,3.4);


/* v10 ambient matrices — slow, low-noise motion shared across product surfaces */
function mountAmbientMatrix(section, seed=1){
  if(!section || section.querySelector(':scope > .ambient-matrix')) return;
  const c=document.createElement('canvas'); c.className='ambient-matrix'; section.prepend(c);
  const ctx=c.getContext('2d'); let w=0,h=0,raf=0;
  function size(){({w,h}=fitCanvas(c,ctx))}
  function frame(){
    const t=performance.now()/1000; ctx.clearRect(0,0,w,h);
    const gap=w<760?26:32; const cols=Math.ceil(w/gap)+2; const rows=Math.ceil(h/gap)+2;
    for(let cc=0;cc<cols;cc++){
      const x=cc*gap+gap*.5;
      const center=.54+.10*Math.sin(t*.045+seed);
      const boundary=h*center + Math.sin(x*.008+t*.055+seed)*h*.05 + Math.sin(x*.017-t*.032+seed*.7)*h*.018;
      for(let rr=0;rr<rows;rr++){
        const y=rr*gap+gap*.5;
        const dist=Math.abs(y-boundary);
        const band=Math.max(0,1-dist/(h*.18));
        const field=Math.max(0,(y-boundary)/(h*.46));
        if(band<.04 && field<.06) continue;
        const hash=(cc*23+rr*41+Math.floor(seed*13))%113;
        let alpha=.035+band*.16+field*.07;
        let fill='#aaa3a2';
        if(hash<8){fill='#e6b5c5';alpha+=.16}
        else if(hash<24){fill='#f2ede7';alpha+=.08}
        const r=1.15+band*2.1+field*1.2+(hash%19===0?.8:0);
        ctx.globalAlpha=alpha;ctx.fillStyle=fill;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,r,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
      }
    }
    ctx.globalAlpha=1;raf=requestAnimationFrame(frame);
  }
  size();frame();addEventListener('resize',size,{passive:true});document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',()=>{if(document.hidden)cancelAnimationFrame(raf);else frame()});
}
[
  ['.home-system',1.1],['.explore-space',2.2],['.launch-space',3.3],['.optimizer-space',4.4],
  ['.rewards-paper',5.5],['.eco-paper',6.6],['.registry-content',7.7],['.coin-detail',8.8]
].forEach(([sel,seed])=>mountAmbientMatrix(document.querySelector(sel),seed));

}

function drawHolderMap(){const c=$('#holderMatrixCanvas');if(!c)return;const ctx=c.getContext('2d');const d=coinData[coinKey]||coinData.nuzero;let w=0,h=0;function draw(){({w,h}=fitCanvas(c,ctx));ctx.clearRect(0,0,w,h);const nodes=[];for(let i=0;i<180;i++){const cluster=i%5;const angle=(i*1.73+d.seed)*.72;const baseR=28+(i%17)*9;const cx=[.28,.53,.72,.44,.62][cluster]*w,cy=[.38,.31,.55,.68,.76][cluster]*h;nodes.push({x:cx+Math.cos(angle)*baseR*.55,y:cy+Math.sin(angle)*baseR*.36,r:2+(i%29===0?8:i%11===0?4:0),kind:i%29===0?'top':i%4===0?'regular':'passive'})}ctx.strokeStyle='#27242b';ctx.globalAlpha=.45;for(let i=0;i<nodes.length;i+=12){for(let j=i+1;j<Math.min(i+5,nodes.length);j++){ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(nodes[i].x,nodes[i].y);ctx.lineTo(nodes[j].x,nodes[j].y);ctx.stroke()}}ctx.globalAlpha=1;nodes.forEach(n=>{ctx.fillStyle=n.kind==='top'?'#e7b5c4':n.kind==='regular'?'#eee9e3':'#565259';ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(n.x,n.y,n.r,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill()})}draw();addEventListener('resize',draw,{passive:true})}
drawHolderMap();

/* guided launch builder */
let builderStep=1;
const stageNames=['IDENTITY','STRUCTURE','LIQUIDITY','AI OPTIMIZE','DISTRIBUTION','MIND','REVIEW','DEPLOY'];
function updateBuilder(){if(!$('.launch-builder-shell'))return;$$('.builder-step').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('active',+b.dataset.step===builderStep));$$('[data-builder-panel]').forEach(p=>p.classList.toggle('active',+p.dataset.builderPanel===builderStep));$('#builderStageLabel').textContent=`0${builderStep} / ${stageNames[builderStep-1]}`;$('#builderProgress').style.width=`${builderStep/8*100}%`;$('#builderPrev').style.visibility=builderStep===1?'hidden':'visible';$('#builderNext').textContent=builderStep===8?'DONE':'CONTINUE →';syncBuilderReview()}
function syncBuilderReview(){if(!$('.launch-builder-shell'))return;const name=$('#launchName')?.value||'NOVA',ticker=$('#launchTicker')?.value||'NVA',creator=+($('#launchCreator')?.value||8),liq=+($('#launchLiquidity')?.value||220),venue=$('#launchVenue')?.value||'RAYDIUM';$('#generatedToken')&&($('#generatedToken').textContent=(ticker[0]||'N').toUpperCase());$('#generatedName')&&($('#generatedName').textContent=`${name.toUpperCase()} / ${ticker.toUpperCase()}`);$('#launchCreatorOut')&&($('#launchCreatorOut').textContent=`${creator}%`);$('#launchLiquidityOut')&&($('#launchLiquidityOut').textContent=`${liq} SOL`);$('#venuePreview')&&($('#venuePreview').textContent=venue);const risk=creator<=8?'LOW':creator<=12?'MODERATE':'HIGH';$('#structureRisk')&&($('#structureRisk').textContent=risk);$('#structureFill')&&($('#structureFill').style.width=`${Math.min(90,18+creator*4)}%`);const score=Math.max(68,Math.min(98,94-Math.max(0,creator-6)*1.1+Math.min(4,(liq-180)/30)));$('#builderScore')&&($('#builderScore').textContent=Math.round(score));$('#builderRisk')&&($('#builderRisk').textContent=risk);$('#builderDepth')&&($('#builderDepth').textContent=liq>=240?'A+':liq>=180?'A':'B');$('#builderAdvice')&&($('#builderAdvice').textContent=score>=92?'STRUCTURE LOOKS HEALTHY.':'MODEL SEES IMPROVEMENT ROOM.');$('#builderAdviceText')&&($('#builderAdviceText').textContent=creator>8?'Creator exposure is the clearest concentration lever.':'Opening depth and creator exposure are inside the modeled target range.');$('#reviewToken')&&($('#reviewToken').textContent=`${name.toUpperCase()} / ${ticker.toUpperCase()}`);$('#reviewLiquidity')&&($('#reviewLiquidity').textContent=`${liq} SOL`);$('#reviewCreator')&&($('#reviewCreator').textContent=`${creator}%`);$('#reviewVenue')&&($('#reviewVenue').textContent=venue);$('#reviewScore')&&($('#reviewScore').textContent=`${Math.round(score)} / 100`)}
$$('.builder-step').forEach(b=>b.addEventListener('click',()=>{builderStep=+b.dataset.step;updateBuilder()}));$('#builderNext')?.addEventListener('click',()=>{if(builderStep<8){builderStep++;updateBuilder()}else showToast('Launch receipt complete')});$('#builderPrev')?.addEventListener('click',()=>{if(builderStep>1){builderStep--;updateBuilder()}});['launchName','launchTicker','launchCreator','launchLiquidity','launchVenue'].forEach(id=>$(`#${id}`)?.addEventListener('input',syncBuilderReview));
$$('[data-ai-apply]').forEach(b=>b.addEventListener('click',()=>{if(b.dataset.aiApply==='creator')$('#launchCreator').value=6;if(b.dataset.aiApply==='liquidity')$('#launchLiquidity').value=260;syncBuilderReview();b.textContent='APPLIED ✓';showToast('Recommendation applied')}));$$('.dist-card,.mind-select').forEach(b=>b.addEventListener('click',()=>b.classList.toggle('selected')));updateBuilder();
if($('#builderMatrix'))drawPulseField($('#builderMatrix'),true,6.2);
let mapIndex=0;const mapTimer=setInterval(()=>{const nodes=$$('.launch-map-card .map-node');if(!nodes.length){clearInterval(mapTimer);return}nodes.forEach((n,i)=>n.classList.toggle('active',i===mapIndex));mapIndex=(mapIndex+1)%nodes.length},1500);
